This closes the gap left after `trace-map-leitstand`\n7\t  shipped only the Trace Map page's OWN in-page banner: an operator on ANY\n8\t  other admin page (Dashboard, Users, Rooms, ...) previously had no way to\n9\t  tell a session was running without navigating to `/admin/logs` or\n10\t  `/admin/logs/trace-map` first.\n11\t\n12\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n13\t  Approved visual reference (amendment 4's own citation):\n14\t  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`\n15\t  — topbar `.pill--warroom` (`\"War Room recording · \" <> mm:ss`) and sidebar\n16\t  `#wr-navdot`.\n17\t\n18\t  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n19\t\n20\t  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) has no\n21\t  `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_session` assign at all today — the topbar\n22\t  `<header>` renders only the static \"Nebu Admin\" brand text, and the\n23\t  sidebar's \"Logs\" section's children (`@logs_nav_children`, including the\n24\t  already-shipped `\"logs-war-room\"` key from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) render\n25\t  via the existing, dot-agnostic `nav_item/1` child markup. Every test below\n26\t  fails because neither `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` nor\n27\t  `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` exist in the rendered markup at\n28\t  all, regardless of the assign passed in.\n29\t\n30\t  ## Contract pinned by this file\n31\t\n32\t    * `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` accepts new assigns (safely defaulted via\n33\t      `assign_new/3` for every OTHER existing LiveView call site that does\n34\t      not set them, mirroring the existing `:active_nav`/\n35\t      `:bootstrap_completed` precedent):\n36\t        - `:war_room_active` (boolean, default `false`)\n37\t        - `:war_room_started_at` (`DateTime.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n38\t          used to compute the topbar pill's elapsed `mm:ss` text\n39\t        - `:war_room_started_by` (`String.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n40\t          optional, for a fuller pill label\n41\t      Amendment 4's own wording: \"sourced from\n42\t      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0`, polled the same way\n43\t      `LogsLive`/`LogsLive.TraceMap` already do\" — this file pins the\n44\t      RENDER contract only; the poll wiring itself is exercised end-to-end\n45\t      by the `@describetag :integration` block below and by\n46\t      `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature`.\n47\t    * Sidebar: `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` renders inside the\n48\t      `<li>` for `[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]` ONLY when\n49\t      `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false` (the default).\n50\t    * Topbar: `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` in the `<header>`\n51\t      renders ONLY when `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false`.\n52\t      When present, it contains the text \"War Room recording\" (per the\n53\t      approved dummy's `.pill--warroom` copy) — the exact `mm:ss` ticking\n54\t      value is client-side/E2E territory (mirrors\n55\t      `trace-map-war-room-elapsed`'s own `data-started-at` + app.js-tick\n56\t      precedent), so this file only pins a `data-started-at` attribute\n57\t      carrying the session's start time for that same client-side tick\n58\t      mechanism to consume.\n59\t    * Both indicators are ADDITIVE — the pre-existing \"Nebu Admin\" brand\n60\t      text and the \"Logs\" section's other children render unchanged\n61\t      regardless of `@war_room_active`.\n62\t  \"\"\"\n63\t  use List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n75\t\n76\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n77\t  # Component-level contract (no DB, no router) — mirrors\n78\t  # trace_map_bubble_test.exs's render_component/2 pattern.\n79\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n80\t\n81\t  describe \"amendment 4: Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1 renders the dot/pill from assigns\" do\n82\t    defp base_assigns(overrides) do\n83\t      Map.merge(\n84\t        %{\n85\t          __changed__: nil,\n86\t          active_nav: \"dashboard\",\n87\t          bootstrap_completed: true,\n88\t          inner_content: \"content\",\n89\t          war_room_active: false,\n90\t          war_room_started_at: nil,\n91\t sidebar recording dot renders on the War Room nav item\" do\n108\t      html =\n109\t        render_component(\n110\t          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n111\t          base_assigns(%{\n112\t Floki.find(war_room_li, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])) != [],\n127\t             \"amendment 4: an active session must show a recording dot on the sidebar's \" <>\n128\t               \"War Room nav item\"\n129\t    end\n130\t\n131\t    test \"with war_room_active: true, the topbar recording pill renders with the expected copy\" do\n132\t      html =\n133\t        render_component(\n134\t          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n135\t          base_assigns(%{\n136\t            war_room_active: true,\n137\t            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 @describetag :integration\n177\t\n178\t    defp admin_conn(path) do\n179\t      %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n180\t      |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n181\t      |> fetch_session()\n182\t      |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n183\t        \"admin\" => true,\n184\t        \"sub\" => \"admin-wr-indicator-tests\",\n185\t        \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n186\t      })\n187\t    end\n188\t\n189\t    setup do\n190\t      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n191\t\n192\t      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n193\t        Nebu.Repo,\n194\t        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n195\t        []\n196\t      )\n197\t\n198\t      :ok\n199\t    end\n200\t\n201\t    test \"visible from /admin/logs (an unrelated admin LiveView page), not just the War Room page itself\" do\n202\t      {:ok, session} =\n203\t        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n204\t          started_by: \"@wr-indicator-tests:nebu.example\",\n205\t          reason: \"amendment 4 dot/pill ATDD probe\",\n206\t          max_entries: 100,\n207\t          max_duration_s: 300\n208\t        })\n209\t\n210\t      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n211\t\n212\t      sent_conn = \"/admin/logs\" |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n213\t      {:ok, _view, html} = live(sent_conn)\n214\t      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)\n215\t\n216\t      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n217\t             \"amendment 4: 'visible from anywhere' — an active session must show the sidebar \" <>\n218\t               \"dot even while viewing the UNRELATED /admin/logs page, not only the dedicated \" <>\n219\t               \"/admin/logs/war-room page\"\n220\t\n221\t      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n222\t             \"amendment 4: the topbar pill must likewise be visible from /admin/logs\"\n223\t    end\n224\t Nebu.Admin.LayoutsWarRoomIndicatorTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`,\n  Story Amendment 4 (2026-07-08) — a pulsing recording dot on the sidebar's\n  \"War Room\" nav item, and a recording pill in the topbar, while a War Room\n  session is active. This closes the gap left after `trace-map-leitstand`\n  shipped only the Trace Map page's OWN in-page banner: an operator on ANY\n  other admin page (Dashboard, Users, Rooms, ...) previously had no way to\n  tell a session was running without navigating to `/admin/logs` or\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map` first.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n  Approved visual reference (amendment 4's own citation):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`\n  — topbar `.pill--warroom` (`\"War Room recording · \" <> mm:ss`) and sidebar\n  `#wr-navdot`.\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) has no\n  `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_session` assign at all today — the topbar\n  `<header>` renders only the static \"Nebu Admin\" brand text, and the\n  sidebar's \"Logs\" section's children (`@logs_nav_children`, including the\n  already-shipped `\"logs-war-room\"` key from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) render\n  via the existing, dot-agnostic `nav_item/1` child markup. Every test below\n  fails because neither `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` nor\n  `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` exist in the rendered markup at\n  all, regardless of the assign passed in.\n\n  ## Contract pinned by this file\n\n    * `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` accepts new assigns (safely defaulted via\n      `assign_new/3` for every OTHER existing LiveView call site that does\n      not set them, mirroring the existing `:active_nav`/\n      `:bootstrap_completed` precedent):\n        - `:war_room_active` (boolean, default `false`)\n        - `:war_room_started_at` (`DateTime.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          used to compute the topbar pill's elapsed `mm:ss` text\n        - `:war_room_started_by` (`String.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          optional, for a fuller pill label\n      Amendment 4's own wording: \"sourced from\n      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0`, polled the same way\n      `LogsLive`/`LogsLive.TraceMap` already do\" — this file pins the\n      RENDER contract only; the poll wiring itself is exercised end-to-end\n      by the `@describetag :integration` block below and by\n      `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature`.\n    * Sidebar: `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` renders inside the\n      `<li>` for `[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]` ONLY when\n      `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false` (the default).\n    * Topbar: `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` in the `<header>`\n      renders ONLY when `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false`.\n      When present, it contains the text \"War Room recording\" (per the\n      approved dummy's `.pill--warroom` copy) — the exact `mm:ss` ticking\n      value is client-side/E2E territory (mirrors\n      `trace-map-war-room-elapsed`'s own `data-started-at` + app.js-tick\n      precedent), so this file only pins a `data-started-at` attribute\n      carrying the session's start time for that same client-side tick\n      mechanism to consume.\n    * Both indicators are ADDITIVE — the pre-existing \"Nebu Admin\" brand\n      text and the \"Logs\" section's other children render unchanged\n      regardless of `@war_room_active`.\n  \"\"\"\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Plug.Test\n  import Plug.Conn\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-wr-indicator-test-key\", 2)\n\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Component-level contract (no DB, no router) — mirrors\n  # trace_map_bubble_test.exs's render_component/2 pattern.\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1 renders the dot/pill from assigns\" do\n    defp base_assigns(overrides) do\n      Map.merge(\n        %{\n          __changed__: nil,\n          active_nav: \"dashboard\",\n          bootstrap_completed: true,\n          inner_content: \"content\",\n          war_room_active: false,\n          war_room_started_at: nil,\n          war_room_started_by: nil\n        },\n        overrides\n      )\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: false (default), neither the nav dot nor the topbar pill render\" do\n      html = render_component(&Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1, base_assigns(%{})) |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no sidebar recording dot\"\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no topbar recording pill\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the sidebar recording dot renders on the War Room nav item\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      war_room_li =\n        html\n        |> Floki.find(~s(li))\n        |> Enum.find(fn li -> Floki.find(li, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"])) != [] end)\n\n      assert war_room_li, \"expected the 'War Room' nav item (data-navkey=\\\"logs-war-room\\\") to exist\"\n\n      assert Floki.find(war_room_li, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: an active session must show a recording dot on the sidebar's \" <>\n               \"War Room nav item\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the topbar recording pill renders with the expected copy\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]))\n      assert pill, \"amendment 4: expected a topbar recording pill while a session is active\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(pill) =~ \"War Room recording\",\n             \"amendment 4: the pill copy must match the approved dummy's \" <>\n               \"'War Room recording · mm:ss' wording\"\n\n      assert attr(pill, \"data-started-at\"),\n             \"amendment 4: the pill must carry a data-started-at attribute for the \" <>\n               \"client-side elapsed-time tick (mirrors trace-map-war-room-elapsed)\"\n    end\n\n    test \"other Logs children and the brand text are unaffected by war_room_active\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{war_room_active: true, war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z]})\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      assert Floki.find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: unrelated sidebar children must render unchanged\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(html) =~ \"Nebu Admin\",\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar brand text must remain, alongside the pill\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Full-stack integration — a genuinely active session (DB-backed)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: a real active session shows the dot/pill on an UNRELATED admin page\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    defp admin_conn(path) do\n      %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n      |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n      |> fetch_session()\n      |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n        \"admin\" => true,\n        \"sub\" => \"admin-wr-indicator-tests\",\n        \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n      })\n    end\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n        []\n      )\n\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"visible from /admin/logs (an unrelated admin LiveView page), not just the War Room page itself\" do\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: \"@wr-indicator-tests:nebu.example\",\n          reason: \"amendment 4 dot/pill ATDD probe\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n\n      sent_conn = \"/admin/logs\" |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n      {:ok, _view, html} = live(sent_conn)\n      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: 'visible from anywhere' — an active session must show the sidebar \" <>\n               \"dot even while viewing the UNRELATED /admin/logs page, not only the dedicated \" <>\n               \"/admin/logs/war-room page\"\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar pill must likewise be visible from /admin/logs\"\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":226,"startLine":1,"totalLines":226}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a7b78771-83e6-452f-aa72-469ace4171cf","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

This file only pins the pure\n  scope-matching contract in `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom`.\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  Neither the `war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope` column (Dev Notes' exist yet.\n\n  Every test that inserts a session with an `exclusive_scope` override fails\n  with a Postgres `column \"exclusive_scope\" of relation \"war_room_sessions\"\n  does not exist` error (the migration does not exist yet — ATDD does not\n  write implementation, including migrations, per this story's own\n  instructions) — this IS the expected RED state, mirroring\n  `war_room_manager_test.exs`'s own \"AC1: war_room_sessions table exists\"\n  precedent (RED because the schema is missing, not because of a bug in\n  this test file).\n\n  The one schema-contract test below (mirroring `war_room_manager_test.exs`'s\n  \"has every column the story specifies\" test) fails on a plain `refute`/\n  `assert` (the column is simply absent from `information_schema.columns`),\n  not a raised error — a cheap, fast signal for \"the migration has not\n  landed yet\" independent of the raw-SQL insert helper below.\n\n  ## Contract pinned by this file\n\n    * `exclusive_scope` is `nil` or a JSON list of route-template strings.\n    * `WarRoom.check/1`'s matching becomes: an entry whose `route_template`\n      is a member of a NON-EMPTY `exclusive_scope` list is NEVER tagged and\n      NEVER counted against the budget (AC10's literal \"if yes, skip — do\n      not tag, do not count\"), REGARDLESS of what `scope`/`filter` would\n      otherwise have matched.\n    * `exclusive_scope: nil` or `exclusive_scope: []` is a pure no-op —\n      100% backward compatible with every existing `scope_matches?/2` /\n      `filter_matches?/2` test in `war_room_test.exs` (no existing test\n      in that file passes an `exclusive_scope` key at all, so this is an\n      purely ADDITIVE branch, never a replacement of the existing checks).\n    * The exclusion check runs BEFORE (and independently of) `scope`/\n      `filter` matching — an excluded route_template is skipped even when\n      `scope: \"all\"` would otherwise match everything, and even when a\n      `filter` would otherwise match the entry's metadata exactly.\n  \"\"\"\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  @moduletag :integration\n\n  setup do\n \"distinctive-wr-excl-test-#{run_id()}\",\n      reason: \"exclusive scope test reason\",\n      scope: nil,\n      filter: nil,\n      exclusive_scope: nil,\n      max_entries: 1000,\n attrs.scope,\n          attrs.filter,\n          attrs.exclusive_scope,\n          attrs.max_entries,\n          attrs.max_duration_s,\n          attrs.started_at,\n          attrs.entries_collected\n Option A)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"schema: war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope (Option A)\" do\n    test \"the column exists, is nullable, and is JSONB-typed\" do\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"\"\"\n          SELECT data_type, is_nullable FROM information_schema.columns\n          WHERE table_name = 'war_room_sessions' AND column_name = 'exclusive_scope'\n          \"\"\",\n          []\n        )\n\n      assert result.rows == [[\"jsonb\", \"YES\"]],\n             \"expected war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope to be a nullable jsonb column \" <>\n               \"(Dev Notes Option A) — got: #{inspect(result.rows)}\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC10 — exclusive scope skips matching entries entirely\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC10: a route_template in a non-empty exclusive_scope is never tagged/counted\" do\n    test \"an entry for the excluded route is NOT tagged, even though scope=\\\"all\\\" would otherwise match everything\",\n         %{war_room: wr} do\n      id =\n        insert_session(\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]\n        )\n\n      load!(wr)\n\n      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\")\n             ),\n             \"AC10: an excluded route_template must never be tagged, regardless of scope\"\n\n      assert session_row(id).entries_collected == 0,\n             \"AC10: an excluded entry must never increment entries_collected either\"\n    end\n\n    test \"an entry for a DIFFERENT (non-excluded) route IS tagged and counted normally\",\n         %{war_room: wr} do\n      id =\n        insert_session(\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]\n        )\n\n      load!(wr)\n\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\")\n             ) == id\n\n      assert session_row(id).entries_collected == 1\n    end\n\n    test \"multiple excluded routes are all honored (not hardcoded to a single literal string)\",\n         %{war_room: wr} do\n      id =\n        insert_session(\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/health\", \"/admin/keepalive\"]\n        )\n\n      load!(wr)\n\n      for route <- [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", \"/health\", \"/admin/keepalive\"] do\n        refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(log_entry(route_template: route)),\n               \"AC10: #{route} is in exclusive_scope and must never be tagged\"\n      end\n\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(log_entry(route_template: \"/admin/rooms\")) == id\n      assert session_row(id).entries_collected == 1\n    end\n\n    test \"exclusive_scope combines with an existing filter (AND semantics unaffected)\", %{\n      war_room: wr\n    } do\n      id =\n        insert_session(\n          scope: \"all\",\n          filter: %{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"},\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\"]\n        )\n\n      load!(wr)\n\n      # excluded route -> never tagged, even with a matching filter.\n      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(\n                 route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\",\n                 metadata: %{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}\n               )\n             )\n\n      # non-excluded route, filter matches -> tagged.\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(\n                 route_template: \"/admin/users\",\n                 metadata: %{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}\n               )\n             ) == id\n\n      # non-excluded route, filter does NOT match -> not tagged (unrelated\n      # to exclusive_scope — the pre-existing AND-semantics contract).\n      refute Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(\n                 route_template: \"/admin/rooms\",\n                 metadata: %{\"user_id\" => \"@bob:localhost\"}\n               )\n             )\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"backward compatibility: exclusive_scope nil/empty is a pure no-op\" do\n    test \"exclusive_scope: nil matches exactly like the pre-existing scope/filter contract\",\n         %{war_room: wr} do\n      id = insert_session(scope: \"all\", exclusive_scope: nil)\n      load!(wr)\n\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\")\n             ) == id\n    end\n\n    test \"exclusive_scope: [] (empty list) matches exactly like nil\", %{war_room: wr} do\n      id = insert_session(scope: \"all\", exclusive_scope: [])\n      load!(wr)\n\n      assert Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.check(\n               log_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\")\n             ) == id\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":301,"startLine":1,"totalLines":301}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d019725f-a372-4645-949a-4d4cbffbce56","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Every test below fails either with a Postgres\n16\t  `column \"exclusive_scope\" does not exist` error (once a naive\n17\t  implementation tries to persist it) or, for the pure-validation tests, a\n18\t  `FunctionClauseError`/wrong-return-shape because the validation branch does\n19\t  not exist in `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` at all.\n20\t\n21\t  ## Contract pinned by this file\n22\t\n23\t    * `start_session/1` accepts an optional `:exclusive_scope` key: `nil`\n24\t      (default, unchanged) or a list of route-template strings.\n25\t    * Dev Notes item 7 (defense-in-depth): the LiveView form's own\n26\t      checkbox/dropdown UI is NOT the trust boundary — a caller invoking\n27\t      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1` DIRECTLY (bypassing the\n28\t      LiveView form entirely, exactly like `war_room_manager_test.exs`'s own\n29\t      \"F-1\" describe block already does for `max_entries`/`max_duration_s`)\n30\t      must still be rejected for a malformed `exclusive_scope`.\n31\t    * AC9 explicitly allows a free-text \"other\" custom route (not just the\n32\t      canonical `long_polling_endpoints/0` members) — so validation here is\n33\t      SHAPE validation (every element is a non-empty string that looks like\n34\t      an absolute route path, i.e. starts with `/`), not a closed allowlist\n35\t      check. This still closes the \"arbitrary non-string/injection payload\"\n36\t      class of defense-in-depth gap Dev Notes item 7 asks for, without\n37\t      contradicting AC9's own free-text allowance.\n38\t    * Every member of `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0`\n39\t      (amendment 5 — the canonical, reused off-list; NOT a hardcoded parallel\n40\t      list) is always accepted.\n41\t    * A rejected `exclusive_scope` returns `{:error, :invalid_params,\n42\t      message}` — same error shape the existing `max_entries`/\n43\t      `max_duration_s` bounds violations already use — and must NOT create\n44\t      any session row (all-or-nothing, mirrors the existing bounds-violation\n45\t      tests).\n46\t  \"\"\"\n47\t  use ExUnit.Case, async: Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManagerExclusiveScopeTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`\n  (AC9/AC10 + Dev Notes item 7 — `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1`\n  accepting and DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH validating an optional `:exclusive_scope`\n  parameter).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `start_session/1` does not accept/persist `:exclusive_scope` at all yet,\n  and the `war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope` column does not exist (see\n  `war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs` for the column's own schema-contract\n  test). Every test below fails either with a Postgres\n  `column \"exclusive_scope\" does not exist` error (once a naive\n  implementation tries to persist it) or, for the pure-validation tests, a\n  `FunctionClauseError`/wrong-return-shape because the validation branch does\n  not exist in `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager` at all.\n\n  ## Contract pinned by this file\n\n    * `start_session/1` accepts an optional `:exclusive_scope` key: `nil`\n      (default, unchanged) or a list of route-template strings.\n    * Dev Notes item 7 (defense-in-depth): the LiveView form's own\n      checkbox/dropdown UI is NOT the trust boundary — a caller invoking\n      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1` DIRECTLY (bypassing the\n      LiveView form entirely, exactly like `war_room_manager_test.exs`'s own\n      \"F-1\" describe block already does for `max_entries`/`max_duration_s`)\n      must still be rejected for a malformed `exclusive_scope`.\n    * AC9 explicitly allows a free-text \"other\" custom route (not just the\n      canonical `long_polling_endpoints/0` members) — so validation here is\n      SHAPE validation (every element is a non-empty string that looks like\n      an absolute route path, i.e. starts with `/`), not a closed allowlist\n      check. This still closes the \"arbitrary non-string/injection payload\"\n      class of defense-in-depth gap Dev Notes item 7 asks for, without\n      contradicting AC9's own free-text allowance.\n    * Every member of `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0`\n      (amendment 5 — the canonical, reused off-list; NOT a hardcoded parallel\n      list) is always accepted.\n    * A rejected `exclusive_scope` returns `{:error, :invalid_params,\n      message}` — same error shape the existing `max_entries`/\n      `max_duration_s` bounds violations already use — and must NOT create\n      any session row (all-or-nothing, mirrors the existing bounds-violation\n      tests).\n  \"\"\"\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  @moduletag :integration\n\n  setup do\n    Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n    on_exit(fn ->\n      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"DELETE FROM war_room_sessions WHERE started_by LIKE 'distinctive-wrm-excl-test-%'\",\n          []\n        )\n      end\n    end)\n\n    :ok\n  end\n\n  defp run_id, do: \"#{System.unique_integer([:positive])}-#{:erlang.phash2(self())}\"\n  defp new_started_by, do: \"distinctive-wrm-excl-test-#{run_id()}\"\n\n  defp end_all_active! do\n    Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n      Nebu.Repo,\n      \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n      []\n    )\n  end\n\n  defp active_session_count do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"SELECT count(*) FROM war_room_sessions WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n        []\n      )\n\n    [[count]] = result.rows\n    count\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Amendment 5: long_polling_endpoints/0 is the canonical source, reused here\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 5: every canonical long-polling endpoint is accepted as an exclusive_scope entry\" do\n    test \"start_session/1 accepts exclusive_scope containing every long_polling_endpoints/0 member\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"exclusive scope with the canonical off-list\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints(),\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert session.exclusive_scope == Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints()\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC3/optional param — persisted as given, omitted defaults to nil\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC9/AC10: exclusive_scope is optional and persisted as given\" do\n    test \"omitted exclusive_scope defaults to nil, unchanged from the pre-existing contract\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"no exclusive_scope given\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert is_nil(session.exclusive_scope)\n    end\n\n    test \"AC9's free-text 'other' custom route (not in the canonical off-list) is accepted when it has route-path shape\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"custom exclusive route via the 'other' input\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/admin/very/custom/noisy-route\"],\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert session.exclusive_scope == [\"/admin/very/custom/noisy-route\"]\n    end\n\n    test \"an empty list is accepted and persisted as an empty list (or nil), never an error\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"empty exclusive_scope list\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [],\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert session.exclusive_scope in [nil, []]\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Dev Notes item 7 — defense-in-depth shape validation, called DIRECTLY\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"Dev Notes item 7: start_session/1 rejects a malformed exclusive_scope, called directly\" do\n    test \"rejects a non-list exclusive_scope (e.g. a bare string)\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      result =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"exclusive_scope is a bare string, not a list\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert {:error, :invalid_params, message} = result\n      assert message =~ \"exclusive_scope\"\n      assert active_session_count() == 0,\n             \"a malformed exclusive_scope must NOT create a session\"\n    end\n\n    test \"rejects a list containing a non-string element\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      result =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"exclusive_scope list has a non-string element\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"/health\", 123],\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert {:error, :invalid_params, message} = result\n      assert message =~ \"exclusive_scope\"\n      assert active_session_count() == 0\n    end\n\n    test \"rejects an entry that does not look like an absolute route path (does not start with '/')\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      result =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"exclusive_scope entry is not a route path — defense-in-depth shape check\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"not-a-route; DROP TABLE war_room_sessions;\"],\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert {:error, :invalid_params, message} = result\n      assert message =~ \"exclusive_scope\"\n      assert active_session_count() == 0,\n             \"the defense-in-depth shape check must reject this even though the LiveView's \" <>\n               \"own checkbox/text-input UI would never produce it directly\"\n    end\n\n    test \"rejects an empty-string entry\" do\n      end_all_active!()\n\n      result =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: new_started_by(),\n          reason: \"exclusive_scope contains a blank entry\",\n          scope: \"all\",\n          exclusive_scope: [\"\"],\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      assert {:error, :invalid_params, _message} = result\n      assert active_session_count() == 0\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":244,"startLine":1,"totalLines":244}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d6c43e94-936d-4a53-86e5-66e47505b300","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Mirrors the epic-wide `war-room-mode`/\n40\t * `trace-map-leitstand`/`trace-map-node-controls` precedent exactly.\n41\t *\n42\t * ## No sleeps, no first-Tab assumptions\n43\t *\n44\t * Session-active/duration-based state is DB-seeded (`startedSecondsAgo`/\n45\t * `endedSecondsAgo` params), never waited-out with `page.waitForTimeout`.\n46\t * Every interaction that depends on the LiveSocket being connected (starting\n47\t * a session, checking an exclusive-scope checkbox) waits for\n48\t * `[data-phx-main].phx-connected` first — the SAME pattern\n49\t * `war-room.steps.ts`'s own \"starts a War Room\" step already establishes\n50\t * for this exact class of race (this page does not need a dedicated\n51\t * `phx-hook` of its own — the exclusive-scope UI and copy-to-markdown both\n52\t * work through plain LiveView `phx-change`/`phx-click` round trips plus the\n53\t * ALREADY-EXISTING generic `nebu:copy-thread` document-level listener in\n54\t * `app.js`; only the sidebar dot / topbar pill client-side tick needs new\n55\t * JS, and that lives in `Nebu.Admin.Layouts`, shared by every admin page —\n56\t * no per-page hook-ready flag to await there either).\n57\t */\n58\t\n59\timport { Given, When, Then } from '../../fixtures/nebu-fixtures';\n60\timport { expect } from '@playwright/test';\n61\timport type { Page } from '@playwright/test';\n62\timport {\n63\t  insertWarRoomSession,\n64\t  insertOperationalLogForWarRoom,\n65\t  endActiveWarRoomSessions,\n66\t  clearWarRoomSessions,\n67\t} from '../../fixtures/db';\n68\t\n69\t// Mirrors bootstrap.steps.ts's own local ADMIN_BASE_URL constant exactly\n70\t// (each step file defines its own copy rather than exporting a shared one —\n71\t// existing convention in this codebase).\n72\tconst ADMIN_BASE_URL = process.env.NEBU_ADMIN_URL ?? 'http://localhost:8008';\n73\t\n74\t// Tracks the session under test across a scenario's Given/When/Then chain:\n75\t// `lastSeededSessionId` is the war_room_sessions.id UUID (needed to tag\n76\t// operational_logs rows correctly), `lastSeededSessionStartedBy` is its\n77\t// human-readable started_by (used for the \"clicks the session row for\n78\t// {string}\" locator, which matches on visible * Step definitions for features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature\n *\n * Story enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page — ATDD, written FIRST.\n * docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n *\n * RED PHASE: every step below targets markup/behavior that does not exist\n * yet (`Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`, the sidebar dot, the topbar pill, the\n * `exclusive_scope` column). The scenarios are `@pending-feature`-tagged\n * (excluded from the default E2E run — `e2e/playwright.config.ts`'s\n * `DEFAULT_E2E_TAGS`) until the dev-story lands them and un-tags this file,\n * mirroring `war-room.feature`'s own dev-story un-tagging convention.\n *\n * Shared background/navigation/recording steps reused from existing step\n * files (this project's one-definition-per-step-text convention — see\n * `logs-config.steps.ts`'s own header comment for the identical note):\n *   - \"the Nebu stack is running\"                 (common/stack-health.steps.ts)\n *   - \"bootstrap has been completed\"               (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator is logged in as admin\"         (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator navigates to {string}\"         (admin/bootstrap.steps.ts)\n *   - \"a request to {string} has been recorded\"    (admin/logs-monitoring.steps.ts)\n *   - \"the operator selects {string} as the War Room scope\"\n *   - \"the operator starts a War Room with reason {string} and scope {string}\"\n *   - \"the operator stops the active War Room\"\n *   - \"the War Room panel shows {string}\"\n *                                                   (admin/war-room.steps.ts)\n *\n * Data-attribute contract this file pins down for the dev agent — see\n * war-room-dedicated-page.feature's header comment for the full list, and\n * server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room_test.exs /\n * server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_war_room_indicator_test.exs for the\n * identical contract pinned down on the ExUnit side.\n *\n * ## No test-only HTTP routes (epic-wide lesson)\n *\n * Every `Given` seed below goes through `fixtures/db.ts`'s direct-SQL\n * helpers (extended for this story: `insertWarRoomSession`,\n * `insertOperationalLogForWarRoom`, `clearWarRoomSessions`) — never a\n * test-only HTTP endpoint. Mirrors the epic-wide `war-room-mode`/\n * `trace-map-leitstand`/`trace-map-node-controls` precedent exactly.\n *\n * ## No sleeps, no first-Tab assumptions\n *\n * Session-active/duration-based state is DB-seeded (`startedSecondsAgo`/\n * `endedSecondsAgo` params), never waited-out with `page.waitForTimeout`.\n * Every interaction that depends on the LiveSocket being connected (starting\n * a session, checking an exclusive-scope checkbox) waits for\n * `[data-phx-main].phx-connected` first — the SAME pattern\n * `war-room.steps.ts`'s own \"starts a War Room\" step already establishes\n * for this exact class of race (this page does not need a dedicated\n * `phx-hook` of its own — the exclusive-scope UI and copy-to-markdown both\n * work through plain LiveView `phx-change`/`phx-click` round trips plus the\n * ALREADY-EXISTING generic `nebu:copy-thread` document-level listener in\n * `app.js`; only the sidebar dot / topbar pill client-side tick needs new\n * JS, and that lives in `Nebu.Admin.Layouts`, shared by every admin page —\n * no per-page hook-ready flag to await there either).\n */\n\nimport { Given, When, Then } from '../../fixtures/nebu-fixtures';\nimport { expect } from '@playwright/test';\nimport type { Page } from '@playwright/test';\nimport {\n  insertWarRoomSession,\n  insertOperationalLogForWarRoom,\n  endActiveWarRoomSessions,\n  clearWarRoomSessions,\n} from '../../fixtures/db';\n\n// Mirrors bootstrap.steps.ts's own local ADMIN_BASE_URL constant exactly\n// (each step file defines its own copy rather than exporting a shared one —\n// existing convention in this codebase).\nconst ADMIN_BASE_URL = process.env.NEBU_ADMIN_URL ?? 'http://localhost:8008';\n\n// Tracks the session under test across a scenario's Given/When/Then chain:\n// `lastSeededSessionId` is the war_room_sessions.id UUID (needed to tag\n// operational_logs rows correctly), `lastSeededSessionStartedBy` is its\n// human-readable started_by (used for the \"clicks the session row for\n// {string}\" locator, which matches on visible row text, not the id).\nlet lastSeededSessionId: string | null = null;\nlet lastSeededSessionStartedBy: string | null = null;\n\nfunction sessionRowLocator(page: Page, startedBy: string) {\n  return page.locator(`[data-testid=\"war-room-session-row\"]:has-text(\"${startedBy}\")`);\n}\n\n// ─── Given: seeding via direct SQL (no test-only HTTP routes) ──────────────\n\nGiven(\n  'a War Room session {string} is active, started {int} seconds ago',\n  async ({}: object, startedBy: string, startedSecondsAgo: number) => {\n    endActiveWarRoomSessions();\n    lastSeededSessionId = insertWarRoomSession({\n      startedBy,\n      reason: 'e2e dedicated-page ATDD probe (active)',\n      startedSecondsAgo,\n    });\n    lastSeededSessionStartedBy = startedBy;\n  },\n);\n\nGiven(\n  'an ended War Room session {string} exists, ended {int} minute(s) ago',\n  async ({}: object, startedBy: string, endedMinutesAgo: number) => {\n    lastSeededSessionId = insertWarRoomSession({\n      startedBy,\n      reason: 'e2e dedicated-page ATDD probe (ended)',\n      startedSecondsAgo: endedMinutesAgo * 60 + 60,\n      endedSecondsAgo: endedMinutesAgo * 60,\n      endedReason: 'manual_stop',\n    });\n    lastSeededSessionStartedBy = startedBy;\n  },\n);\n\nGiven('no War Room sessions exist', async () => {\n  clearWarRoomSessions();\n});\n\nGiven('that session has {int} captured log entries', async ({}: object, count: number) => {\n  if (!lastSeededSessionId) {\n    throw new Error('[war-room-dedicated-page] no War Room session has been seeded in this scenario');\n  }\n\n  // AC5: the detail page filters strictly by war_room_id — tag every seeded\n  // entry with the REAL session UUID returned by insertWarRoomSession, not\n  // the human-readable started_by string.\n  const sessionId = lastSeededSessionId;\n  const startedBy = lastSeededSessionStartedBy;\n  for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {\n    insertOperationalLogForWarRoom(sessionId, {\n      message: `e2e-seeded captured entry ${i + 1} for ${startedBy}`,\n    });\n  }\n});\n\n// ─── When: interactions specific to this page ──────────────────────────────\n\nWhen('the operator clicks the session row for {string}', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, startedBy: string) => {\n  await sessionRowLocator(page, startedBy).click();\n});\n\nWhen('the operator navigates to a non-existent War Room detail page', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await page.goto(`${ADMIN_BASE_URL}/admin/logs/war-room/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000`);\n});\n\nWhen('the operator checks {string} as an excluded endpoint', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, route: string) => {\n  await page\n    .locator(`[data-testid=\"war-room-exclusive-options\"] input[type=\"checkbox\"][value=\"${route}\"]`)\n    .check();\n});\n\nWhen('the operator clicks the copy-to-markdown button', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  // Clipboard writes require a granted permission — see the assertion step\n  // below for the matching `clipboard-read` grant (both are requested per\n  // scenario, mirroring standard Playwright clipboard-testing practice).\n  await page.context().grantPermissions(['clipboard-read', 'clipboard-write']);\n  await page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-copy-markdown-button\"]').click();\n});\n\n// ─── Then: list view ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nThen('the War Room session list shows {int} sessions', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, count: number) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-session-row\"]')).toHaveCount(count, {\n    timeout: 10_000,\n  });\n});\n\nThen('the first session row shows the {string} status badge', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, badgeText: string) => {\n  const firstRow = page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-session-row\"]').first();\n  await expect(firstRow.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\"]')).toContainText(badgeText);\n});\n\nThen('a session row shows the {string} status badge', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, badgeText: string) => {\n  await expect(\n    page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\"]', { hasText: badgeText }).first(),\n  ).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the War Room empty-state message is visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-empty-state\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen(\n  'the War Room empty-state message mentions {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, expectedSubstring: string) => {\n    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-empty-state\"]')).toContainText(expectedSubstring);\n  },\n);\n\n// ─── Then: detail view ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nThen(\"the operator is on the War Room session's detail page\", async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\\/admin\\/logs\\/war-room\\/[0-9a-f-]{36}/, { timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen(\n  'the War Room detail metadata panel shows {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, expectedSubstring: string) => {\n    await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-detail-meta\"]')).toContainText(expectedSubstring, {\n      timeout: 10_000,\n    });\n  },\n);\n\nThen('the War Room detail page shows {int} captured log entries', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, count: number) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"request-row\"]')).toHaveCount(count, { timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the War Room not-found message is visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-not-found\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the operator can navigate back to the War Room list from there', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-detail-back-link\"]').click();\n  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\\/admin\\/logs\\/war-room$/, { timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\n// ─── Then: scoping form / live preview / exclusive scope ───────────────────\n\nThen('the War Room start form is visible on the dedicated page', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the War Room live-activity preview is visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-trace-preview\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the War Room live-activity preview updates without a page reload', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  const preview = page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-trace-preview\"]');\n  await expect(\n    preview.locator('[data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"][data-bubble-dimension=\"source\"][data-bubble-value=\"http\"]'),\n  ).not.toHaveAttribute('data-bubble-count', '0', { timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the War Room exclusive-scope options are visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-exclusive-options\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen(\n  'the War Room exclusive-scope options include {string}',\n  async ({ page }: { page: Page }, route: string) => {\n    await expect(\n      page.locator(`[data-testid=\"war-room-exclusive-options\"] input[type=\"checkbox\"][value=\"${route}\"]`),\n    ).toBeVisible();\n  },\n);\n\n// ─── Then: amendment 2 — embedded panel retirement ─────────────────────────\n\nThen('the embedded War Room panel is not present on the page', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-panel\"]')).toHaveCount(0);\n});\n\nThen('a link to the dedicated War Room page is visible on {string}', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, _path: string) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('a[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\n// ─── Then: amendment 4 — nav dot + topbar pill ─────────────────────────────\n\nThen('the sidebar War Room recording dot is visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the topbar War Room recording pill is visible', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10_000 });\n});\n\nThen('the topbar War Room recording pill shows an elapsed time', async ({ page }: { page: Page }) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]')).toContainText(/\\d{1,2}:\\d{2}/, {\n    timeout: 10_000,\n  });\n});\n\n// ─── Then: AC14 — copy-to-markdown ──────────────────────────────────────────\n\nThen('the operator sees a {string} confirmation', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, message: string) => {\n  await expect(page.locator('[data-testid=\"war-room-copy-toast\"]')).toContainText(message, {\n    timeout: 10_000,\n  });\n});\n\nThen('the clipboard contains {string}', async ({ page }: { page: Page }, expectedSubstring: string) => {\n  const clipboardText = await page.evaluate(() => navigator.clipboard.readText());\n  expect(clipboardText).toContain(expectedSubstring);\n});\n","numLines":286,"startLine":1,"totalLines":286}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"83cb7f23-3e93-4c3b-a521-fab883d17533","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"b9ec935d-b6f0-455c-88a5-7217e1346d80","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"6ec863c2-24c3-45ae-8423-8a0d3113b5a0","agentId":"ae43d957caaab97ca","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JPTqkvwcVuckvaVMruP6r1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Public API for War Room session lifecycle (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode,\n4\t  AC1/AC2/AC3/AC6/AC10) — `start_session/1`, `stop_session/1`, `status/0`.\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n7\t  Design decisions (see #6 for why there is deliberately NO HTTP\n8\t  controller/route here):\n9\t  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n10\t\n11\t  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — no\n12\t  `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge at all, directly unit-testable without an\n13\t  HTTP round trip. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `handle_event(\"start_war_room\" /\n14\t  \"stop_war_room\", ...)` clauses call straight into these functions.\n15\t\n16\t  ## AC2 — \"exactly one active session\"\n17\t\n18\t  This module performs the app-level \"is one already active?\" check first\n19\t  (to return a friendly, human-readable conflict message naming the\n20\t  existing session's `started_by`/`reason`), but the REAL correctness\n21\t  backstop is the migration's partial unique index\n22\t  (`war_room_sessions_single_active_idx`) — a genuinely raced concurrent\n23\t  INSERT still fails there and is mapped to the same `{:error, :conflict,\n24\t  _}` shape.\n25\t\n26\t  Restarting the SAME session (identical `started_by`/`reason`/`scope`/\n27\t  `filter`/`max_entries`/`max_duration_s`) while it is still active is\n28\t  treated as idempotent: `{:ok, existing_session}` is returned without a\n29\t  second row being created.\n30\t\n31\t  ## Cache invalidation\n32\t\n33\t  Every write here (`start_session/1`'s successful insert, `stop_session/1`)\n34\t  calls `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` afterward — mirroring\n35\t  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0` —\n36\t  so the hot-path cache (used by `Handler.log/2` via `WarRoom.check/1`)\n37\t  reflects the change on the very next log entry, not after some future\n38\t  poll.\n39\t  \"\"\"\n40\t\n41\t  @required_params [:started_by, :reason, :max_entries, :max_duration_s]\n42\t\n43\t  # Security review (Kassandra, 2026-07-06, F-1 MEDIUM): these are the SAME\n44\t  # bounds `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `parse_int_bounded/5` already enforces at\n45\t  # the UI layer (AC8/AC11) — duplicated here so the actual trust boundary\n46\t  # (this module's public API, the only thing a caller MUST go through to\n47\t  # create a session) enforces the flooding-safety invariant regardless of\n48\t  # caller, not only when going through the LiveView form. This is\n49\t  # defense-in-depth, the same pattern already used for AC2's \"exactly one\n50\t  # active\" (app-level check + DB-level partial unique index backstop) — the\n51\t  # LiveView's own bounds check stays in place as the friendly\n52\t  # early-UI-feedback layer and is NOT removed/weakened by this.\n53\t  @max_entries_bounds {10, 10_000}\n54\t  @max_duration_s_bounds {10, 3_600}\n55\t\n56\t  @doc \"\"\"\n57\t  Starts a new War Room session with `params` (a map with atom keys):\n58\t\n59\t    * required: `:started_by`, `:reason`, `:max_entries`, `:max_duration_s`\n60\t    * optional: `:scope` (defaults to `nil`, treated as \"all\"), `:filter`\n61\t      (a map, e.g. `%{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}`, defaults to `nil`)\n62\t\n63\t  Returns:\n64\t\n65\t    * `{:ok, session}` — a freshly created session, OR the existing active\n66\t      session when `params` exactly matches it (idempotent restart, AC2).\n67\t    * `{:error, :conflict, message}` — a DIFFERENT session is already\n68\t      active (AC2).\n69\t    * `{:error, :invalid_params, message}` — a required parameter is\n70\t      missing (AC3 — no acceptance test pins the exact shape of this error;\n71\t      this is the chosen, documented contract), OR `:max_entries`/\n72\t      `:max_duration_s` is outside the AC8/AC11 bounds (`10..10_000` /\n73\t      `10..3_600` — security review F-1: this is the actual trust boundary,\n74\t      not just the LiveView form's own bounds check).\n75\t  \"\"\"\n76\t  @spec start_session(map()) ::\n77\t          {:ok, map()} | {:error, :conflict, String.t()} | {:error, :invalid_params, String.t()}\n78\t  def start_session(params) when is_map(params) do\n79\t    with :ok <- validate_required(params) do\n80\t      case fetch_active() do\n81\t        nil ->\n82\t          insert_session(params)\n83\t\n84\t        existing ->\n85\t          if same_session?(existing, params) do\n86\t            {:ok, existing}\n87\t          else\n88\t            {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n89\t          end\n90\t      end\n91\t    end\n92\t  end\n93\t\n94\t  @doc \"\"\"\n95\t  Manually ends session `id` (AC6): sets `ended_at = now()`, Safe no-op when the process\n331\t  # is not currently running.\n332\t  defp notify_war_room_reload do\n333\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom) do\n334\t      Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active()\n335\t    end\n336\t\n337\t    :ok\n338\t  end\n339\tend\n340\t"}]},"uuid":"a481d583-6a29-4ea5-92d8-e12f90f65b6c","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:26:40.532Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Public API for War Room session lifecycle (enhanced-logging/war-room-mode,\n  AC1/AC2/AC3/AC6/AC10) — `start_session/1`, `stop_session/1`, `status/0`.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-mode.md\n  Design decisions (see #6 for why there is deliberately NO HTTP\n  controller/route here):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/step-results/war-room-mode/design-decisions.md`\n\n  A pure-functional, inline-SQL Admin-domain module (ADR-018/020) — no\n  `Plug.Conn`/controller knowledge at all, directly unit-testable without an\n  HTTP round trip. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `handle_event(\"start_war_room\" /\n  \"stop_war_room\", ...)` clauses call straight into these functions.\n\n  ## AC2 — \"exactly one active session\"\n\n  This module performs the app-level \"is one already active?\" check first\n  (to return a friendly, human-readable conflict message naming the\n  existing session's `started_by`/`reason`), but the REAL correctness\n  backstop is the migration's partial unique index\n  (`war_room_sessions_single_active_idx`) — a genuinely raced concurrent\n  INSERT still fails there and is mapped to the same `{:error, :conflict,\n  _}` shape.\n\n  Restarting the SAME session (identical `started_by`/`reason`/`scope`/\n  `filter`/`max_entries`/`max_duration_s`) while it is still active is\n  treated as idempotent: `{:ok, existing_session}` is returned without a\n  second row being created.\n\n  ## Cache invalidation\n\n  Every write here (`start_session/1`'s successful insert, `stop_session/1`)\n  calls `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom.load_active/0` afterward — mirroring\n  `Nebu.Admin.DB.upsert_server_config/1` -> `Nebu.Web.LogStore.refresh_policy_cache/0` —\n  so the hot-path cache (used by `Handler.log/2` via `WarRoom.check/1`)\n  reflects the change on the very next log entry, not after some future\n  poll.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  @required_params [:started_by, :reason, :max_entries, :max_duration_s]\n\n  # Security review (Kassandra, 2026-07-06, F-1 MEDIUM): these are the SAME\n  # bounds `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s `parse_int_bounded/5` already enforces at\n  # the UI layer (AC8/AC11) — duplicated here so the actual trust boundary\n  # (this module's public API, the only thing a caller MUST go through to\n  # create a session) enforces the flooding-safety invariant regardless of\n  # caller, not only when going through the LiveView form. This is\n  # defense-in-depth, the same pattern already used for AC2's \"exactly one\n  # active\" (app-level check + DB-level partial unique index backstop) — the\n  # LiveView's own bounds check stays in place as the friendly\n  # early-UI-feedback layer and is NOT removed/weakened by this.\n  @max_entries_bounds {10, 10_000}\n  @max_duration_s_bounds {10, 3_600}\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Starts a new War Room session with `params` (a map with atom keys):\n\n    * required: `:started_by`, `:reason`, `:max_entries`, `:max_duration_s`\n    * optional: `:scope` (defaults to `nil`, treated as \"all\"), `:filter`\n      (a map, e.g. `%{\"user_id\" => \"@alice:localhost\"}`, defaults to `nil`)\n\n  Returns:\n\n    * `{:ok, session}` — a freshly created session, OR the existing active\n      session when `params` exactly matches it (idempotent restart, AC2).\n    * `{:error, :conflict, message}` — a DIFFERENT session is already\n      active (AC2).\n    * `{:error, :invalid_params, message}` — a required parameter is\n      missing (AC3 — no acceptance test pins the exact shape of this error;\n      this is the chosen, documented contract), OR `:max_entries`/\n      `:max_duration_s` is outside the AC8/AC11 bounds (`10..10_000` /\n      `10..3_600` — security review F-1: this is the actual trust boundary,\n      not just the LiveView form's own bounds check).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec start_session(map()) ::\n          {:ok, map()} | {:error, :conflict, String.t()} | {:error, :invalid_params, String.t()}\n  def start_session(params) when is_map(params) do\n    with :ok <- validate_required(params) do\n      case fetch_active() do\n        nil ->\n          insert_session(params)\n\n        existing ->\n          if same_session?(existing, params) do\n            {:ok, existing}\n          else\n            {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n          end\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Manually ends session `id` (AC6): sets `ended_at = now()`, `ended_reason\n  = \"manual_stop\"`.\n\n  Returns:\n\n    * `:ok` — the session was active and is now ended.\n    * `{:error, :not_found}` — no session with this `id` exists.\n    * `{:error, :already_ended}` — the session exists but was already\n      ended (by budget/duration/a previous manual stop) — a safe, clearly\n      defined no-op, never a crash.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec stop_session(String.t()) :: :ok | {:error, :not_found | :already_ended}\n  def stop_session(id) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' \" <>\n          \"WHERE id = $1 AND ended_at IS NULL\",\n        [id]\n      )\n\n    case result.num_rows do\n      1 ->\n        notify_war_room_reload()\n        :ok\n\n      0 ->\n        case fetch_by_id(id) do\n          nil -> {:error, :not_found}\n          _existing -> {:error, :already_ended}\n        end\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Returns `{:ok, session}` for the currently active session, or `:none`\n  when no session is active.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec status() :: {:ok, map()} | :none\n  def status do\n    case fetch_active() do\n      nil -> :none\n      session -> {:ok, session}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ── validation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp validate_required(params) do\n    missing = Enum.filter(@required_params, fn key -> blank?(Map.get(params, key)) end)\n\n    if missing == [] do\n      validate_bounds(params)\n    else\n      {:error, :invalid_params,\n       \"missing required war room parameter(s): #{Enum.map_join(missing, \", \", &to_string/1)}\"}\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp blank?(nil), do: true\n  defp blank?(\"\"), do: true\n  defp blank?(_), do: false\n\n  # Security review (Kassandra, F-1 MEDIUM): the actual trust-boundary bounds\n  # check — see the @max_entries_bounds/@max_duration_s_bounds moduledoc\n  # comment above. Runs AFTER required-param presence validation (so a\n  # missing param still reports the more specific \"missing\" error first).\n  defp validate_bounds(params) do\n    {min_entries, max_entries_bound} = @max_entries_bounds\n    {min_duration, max_duration_bound} = @max_duration_s_bounds\n\n    with :ok <-\n           validate_in_range(\n             Map.fetch!(params, :max_entries),\n             \"max_entries\",\n             min_entries,\n             max_entries_bound\n           ),\n         :ok <-\n           validate_in_range(\n             Map.fetch!(params, :max_duration_s),\n             \"max_duration_s\",\n             min_duration,\n             max_duration_bound\n           ) do\n      :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp validate_in_range(value, _field_name, min, max)\n       when is_integer(value) and value >= min and value <= max do\n    :ok\n  end\n\n  defp validate_in_range(value, field_name, min, max) do\n    {:error, :invalid_params,\n     \"#{field_name} must be an integer between #{min} and #{max} (got #{inspect(value)})\"}\n  end\n\n  # ── writes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp insert_session(params) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        INSERT INTO war_room_sessions\n          (started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries, max_duration_s)\n        VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)\n        RETURNING id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries, max_duration_s,\n                  started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        \"\"\",\n        [\n          Map.fetch!(params, :started_by),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :reason),\n          Map.get(params, :scope),\n          Map.get(params, :filter),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :max_entries),\n          Map.fetch!(params, :max_duration_s)\n        ]\n      )\n\n    [row] = result.rows\n    session = row_to_session(row)\n    notify_war_room_reload()\n    {:ok, session}\n  rescue\n    e in Postgrex.Error ->\n      if unique_violation?(e) do\n        # A genuinely raced concurrent start won this INSERT before we did —\n        # the app-level \"is one active?\" check above already passed, but\n        # the DB-level partial unique index is the real backstop\n        # (design-decisions.md #8).\n        case fetch_active() do\n          nil -> {:error, :conflict, \"War Room already active — could not start a new session\"}\n          existing -> {:error, :conflict, conflict_message(existing)}\n        end\n      else\n        reraise e, __STACKTRACE__\n      end\n  end\n\n  defp unique_violation?(%Postgrex.Error{postgres: %{code: :unique_violation}}), do: true\n  defp unique_violation?(_), do: false\n\n  # ── idempotent-restart comparison (AC2) ─────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp same_session?(existing, params) do\n    existing.started_by == Map.get(params, :started_by) and\n      existing.reason == Map.get(params, :reason) and\n      existing.scope == Map.get(params, :scope) and\n      existing.filter == Map.get(params, :filter) and\n      existing.max_entries == Map.get(params, :max_entries) and\n      existing.max_duration_s == Map.get(params, :max_duration_s)\n  end\n\n  defp conflict_message(existing) do\n    \"War Room already active, started by #{existing.started_by} at #{existing.started_at}, \" <>\n      \"reason: #{existing.reason} — end it first\"\n  end\n\n  # ── reads ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  defp fetch_active do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries, max_duration_s,\n               started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        FROM war_room_sessions\n        WHERE ended_at IS NULL\n        LIMIT 1\n        \"\"\",\n        []\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n      [] -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp fetch_by_id(id) do\n    result =\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"\"\"\n        SELECT id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, max_entries, max_duration_s,\n               started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\n        FROM war_room_sessions\n        WHERE id = $1\n        \"\"\",\n        [id]\n      )\n\n    case result.rows do\n      [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n      [] -> nil\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp row_to_session([\n         id,\n         started_by,\n         reason,\n         scope,\n         filter,\n         max_entries,\n         max_duration_s,\n         started_at,\n         ended_at,\n         ended_reason,\n         entries_collected\n       ]) do\n    %{\n      id: id,\n      started_by: started_by,\n      reason: reason,\n      scope: scope,\n      filter: filter,\n      max_entries: max_entries,\n      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n      started_at: started_at,\n      ended_at: ended_at,\n      ended_reason: ended_reason,\n      entries_collected: entries_collected\n    }\n  end\n\n  # ── cache invalidation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  # Best-effort: forces Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom to reload the active\n  # session from the DB immediately — mirrors\n  # {"parentUuid":"b83f4007-7165-4cc0-ba4b-6bd3c26bc62a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"6ec863c2-24c3-45ae-8423-8a0d3113b5a0","agentId":"ae43d957caaab97ca","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SeMsAoVyyanTvAMXbUQ6aD","type":"tool_result","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Reusable Trace Map \"bubble\" component (enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view,\n  AC1/AC2/AC5/AC13/AC14/AC16).\n\n  A single toggleable pill-shaped button that is BOTH legend (shows a live count)\n  and interactive filter. Renders identically regardless of `mode` — the component\n  itself has NO opinion on what clicking it does server-side; it simply wires\n  whatever `on_click` value the caller passes onto the rendered `<button>`'s\n  `phx-click` attribute. That is the entire \"designed for reuse\" contract (see\n  `test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble_test.exs`, Acceptance Test 11):\n\n    * `mode: :filter` (THIS story, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`) — the caller passes a\n      `Phoenix.LiveView.JS` command list as `on_click` (client-side DOM toggle +\n      a `nebu:trace-bubble-click` custom-event dispatch picked up by\n      `priv/static/assets/app.js` — see that file's `wireTraceMapFilters`).\n      AC3 requires this to be genuinely client-side-only (no `handle_event`\n      server round-trip per click), so `on_click` here is NEVER a bare event-name\n      string in real `LogsLive` usage.\n    * `mode: :configure` (sibling `admin-logs-retention-settings` story, REUSING\n      this same component) — that story passes a bare event-name string as\n      `on_click` (a normal `phx-click=\"configure_retention\"` push), since\n      AC3's client-side-only requirement is scoped to `:filter` mode only.\n\n  ## Accessibility (AC13/AC14)\n\n  Every bubble is a native `<button>` (natural Tab order, Space/Enter activation),\n  carries `aria-pressed` (true/false) and a descriptive `aria-label` — never a\n  `<div onclick>`. Level indication pairs an icon with text (never color alone).\n\n  ## \"Off\" (uncollected) bubbles (AC2)\n\n  When `off: true`, the bubble renders a distinct \"⚫ off\" visual marker\n  (`data-bubble-off=\"true\"`) instead of a live count — this represents a\n  STRUCTURAL absence (the level/source is not currently being collected per the\n  retention policy), not \"zero events in the active window\".\n  \"\"\"\n  use Phoenix.Component\n\n  attr(:dimension, :atom, required: true, doc: \"One of :endpoint, :level, :source\")\n\n  attr(:value, :string,\n    required: true,\n    doc: \"The specific value, e.g. \\\"/login\\\", \\\"error\\\", \\\"ecto\\\"\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:count, :integer, required: true, doc: \"Live count of matching events in the current view\")\n  attr(:active, :boolean, default: false, doc: \"Whether this filter is currently active\")\n  attr(:mode, :atom, default: :filter, values: [:filter, :configure])\n\n  attr(:on_click, :any,\n    required: true,\n    doc:\n      \"phx-click value: a Phoenix.LiveView.JS command list (:filter) or a bare event name (:configure)\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:off, :boolean,\n    default: false,\n    doc: \"True when this dimension value is not currently collected (AC2)\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:id, :string, default: nil)\n  attr(:label, :string, default: nil, doc: \"Override the default auto-generated label\")\n\n  def trace_map_bubble(assigns) do\n    assigns =\n      assigns\n      |> assign_new(:label, fn -> default_label(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n      |> assign_new(:id, fn -> bubble_dom_id(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      id={@id}\n      data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"\n      data-bubble-dimension={to_string(@dimension)}\n      data-bubble-value={@value}\n      data-bubble-count={to_string(@count)}\n      data-bubble-off={to_string(@off)}\n      aria-pressed={to_string(@active)}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f2200e4a-269b-4485-8927-fe10875d0c50","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:26:52.090Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"defmodule The navkey `logs-config` and the\n55\t  # /admin/logs/config route are unchanged; only the label.\n56\t  @logs_nav_children [\n57\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs\", key: \"logs-monitoring\", label: \"Live Stream\"},\n58\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", key: \"logs-trace-map\", label: \"Trace Map\"},\n59\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/war-room\", key: \"logs-war-room\", label: \"War Room\"},\n60\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Log Retention\"}\n61\t  ]\n62\t\n63\t  # NOTE on ordering: the Leitstand spec (§1) lists \"General\" first, but the\n64\t  # keyboard-nav acceptance contract pins \"Role Mapping\" as the very next Tab\n65\t  # stop after the Configuration toggle (admin-sidebar-nesting.feature,\n66\t  # story Acceptance Test 8) and story AC5 enumerates exactly Role Mapping +\n67\t  # Claim Mapping as the children. \"General\" is APPENDED (not prepended) so\n68\t  # `/admin/config` stays reachable from the nav at all — the parent itself is\n69\t  # a pure disclosure toggle (<button>, AC9), no longer a link.\n70\t  @config_nav_children [\n71\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/role-mapping\", key: \"role-mapping\", label: \"Role Mapping\"},\n72\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/claim-mapping\", key: \"claim-mapping\", label: \"Claim Mapping\"},\n73\t    %{navigate: \"/admin/config\", key: \"config-general\", label: \"General\"}\n74\t  ]\n75\t\n76\t  # The shared admin shell: topbar + sidebar + content. Nav items mirror the Go admin.\n77\t  # Works for both LiveView sockets (assign_new/3 with change tracking) and dead\n78\t  # controller renders (plain map — use Map.put_new/3 for compatibility).\n79\t  def live(assigns) do\n80\t    assigns =\n81\t      if is_map_key(assigns, :__changed__) do\n82\t        assigns\n83\t        |> assign_new(:active_nav, fn -> nil end)\n84\t        |> assign_new(:bootstrap_completed, fn -> true end)\n85\t      else\n86\t        assigns\n87\t        |> Map.put_new(:active_nav, nil)\n88\t        |> Map.put_new(:bootstrap_completed, true)\n89\t class=\"hidden\"\n97\t      aria-hidden=\"true\"\n98\t    >\n99\t    </div>\n100\t    <div class=\"min-h-screen flex flex-col\">\n101\t      <header class=\"h-12 bg-base-300 flex items-center justify-between px-4 shrink-0\" role=\"banner\">\n102\t        <span class=\"font-semibold text-base-content\">Nebu Admin</span>\n103\t      </header>\n104\t\n105\t      <div class=\"flex flex-1
