logs_nav_children, do: @logs_nav_children\n142\t  defp config_nav_children, do: @config_nav_children\n143\t\n144\t  # A single sidebar nav entry, in one of two modes (admin-sidebar-nesting):\n145\t  #\n146\t  #   * LEAF (`children == []`, the default) — exactly the pre-nesting markup:\n147\t  #     a plain `<li><.link data-navkey=...>`. Zero behavior change for every\n148\t  #     existing call site that does not pass `:children` (AC2).\n149\t  #\n150\t  #   * PARENT (`children != []`) — an accessible disclosure group (AC9/AC10):\n151\t  #     a real `<button aria-expanded aria-controls>` toggle (with a chevron\n152\t  #     icon, AC13) followed by a nested, indented `<ul id=\"nav-{key}-children\">`\n153\t  #     of child links. Children ALWAYS stay in the DOM; collapsing only adds\n154\t  #     the boolean `hidden` attribute. The child `<ul>` must NOT carry any\n155\t  #     display utility class (`flex`, `grid`, ...): an author-stylesheet\n156\t  #     `.flex { display: flex }` rule beats the UA's `[hidden] { display:\n157\t  #     none }` in the cascade and the collapsed state would not actually hide\n158\t  #     (the exact trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 bug — do not reintroduce it).\n159\t  #\n160\t  # Highlight contract (AC3/AC4):\n161\t  #   * `key == active`            → primary highlight (leaf and parent alike)\n162\t  #   * a child's `key == active`  → the child gets primary + aria-current=\"page\";\n163\t  #                                  the parent gets the SECONDARY `bg-base-300`\n164\t  #                                  accent (never `bg-primary`)\n165\t  #   * otherwise                  → neutral (hover accent only)\n166\t  #\n167\t  # The expand/collapse behavior is wired CSP-safely (`script-src 'self'`, no\n168\t  # inline JS) by `wireNavSectionToggles()` in priv/static/assets/app.js: a\n169\t  # single delegated click listener flips `aria-expanded` + `hidden` on the\n170\t  # native <button> — clicks, Space and Enter all fire `click` on a native\n171\t  # button, and the delegation works identically on dead controller renders\n172\t  # and LiveView pages. Sections default to EXPANDED (story Dev Notes Q3);\n173\t  # collapse state deliberately does not persist across navigations (Q1).\n174\t  #\n175\t  # `:navigate` is LEAF-ONLY (code-review MINOR-3): a parent renders as a pure\n176\t  # disclosure <button> that toggles and never navigates (AC9 + the keyboard\n177\t  # ATDD contract — Space must flip aria-expanded WITHOUT leaving the page),\n178\t  # so it has no use for a target. The attr is optional so parent call sites\n179\t  # don't pass a meaningless value; every LEAF call site must still pass it —\n180\t  # a leaf without `:navigate` would fall through to Phoenix.Component.link/1's\n181\t  # dead `href=\"#\"` default.\n182\t  attr :navigate, :string, default: nil\n183\t  attr :key, :string, required: true\n184\t  attr :active, :string, default: nil\n185\t  attr :children, :list, default: []\n186\t  attr :expanded, :boolean, default: true\n187\t  slot :inner_block\n188\t\n189\t  def nav_item(%{children: []} = assigns) do\n190\t class={[\n197\t          \"flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm\",\n198\t          \"focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-base-200\",\n199\t          if(@key == @active,\n200\t            do: \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\",\n201\t            else: \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n202\t          )\n203\t        ]}\n204\t      >\n205\t        {render_slot(@inner_block)}\n206\t      </.link>\n207\t    </li>\n208\t    \"\"\"\n209\t  end\n210\t\n211\t  def nav_item(assigns) do\n212\t    highlight =\n213\t      cond do\n214\t        assigns.key == assigns.active -> \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\"\n215\t        Enum.any?(assigns.children, &(&1.key == assigns.active)) -> \"bg-base-300 font-medium text-base-content\"\n216\t        true -> \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n217\t      end\n218\t\n219\t    assigns = assign(assigns, :parent_highlight, highlight)\n220\t\n221\t    ~H\"\"\"\n222\t    <li>\n223\t      <button\n224\t        type=\"button\"\n225\t do: \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\",\n261\t                else: \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n262\t              )\n263\t            ]}\n264\t  end\n\n  # Sidebar IA (enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting + Trace Map \"Leitstand\"\n  # design spec §1). Child keys are parent-namespaced (AC8); `role-mapping` /\n  # `claim-mapping` keep their historical un-namespaced keys so pre-existing\n  # E2E selectors like `[data-navkey=\"role-mapping\"]` keep resolving (AC11).\n  #\n  # `/admin/logs/trace-map` is the real Leitstand page since\n  # `trace-map-leitstand`; `/admin/logs/war-room` remains a stub route (it\n  # renders the existing logs page) until `war-room-dedicated-page` lands.\n  #\n  # trace-map-leitstand AC14 (UX gate advisory A1, decided by Phil\n  # 2026-07-07): the logs-config child is labeled \"Log Retention\" — NOT the\n  # bare \"Configuration\", which duplicated the top-level Configuration\n  # section's accessible name. The navkey `logs-config` and the\n  # /admin/logs/config route are unchanged; only the label.\n  @logs_nav_children [\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs\", key: \"logs-monitoring\", label: \"Live Stream\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", key: \"logs-trace-map\", label: \"Trace Map\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/war-room\", key: \"logs-war-room\", label: \"War Room\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Log Retention\"}\n  ]\n\n  # NOTE on ordering: the Leitstand spec (§1) lists \"General\" first, but the\n  # keyboard-nav acceptance contract pins \"Role Mapping\" as the very next Tab\n  # stop after the Configuration toggle (admin-sidebar-nesting.feature,\n  # story Acceptance Test 8) and story AC5 enumerates exactly Role Mapping +\n  # Claim Mapping as the children. \"General\" is APPENDED (not prepended) so\n  # `/admin/config` stays reachable from the nav at all — the parent itself is\n  # a pure disclosure toggle (<button>, AC9), no longer a link.\n  @config_nav_children [\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/role-mapping\", key: \"role-mapping\", label: \"Role Mapping\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/config/claim-mapping\", key: \"claim-mapping\", label: \"Claim Mapping\"},\n    %{navigate: \"/admin/config\", key: \"config-general\", label: \"General\"}\n  ]\n\n  # The shared admin shell: topbar + sidebar + content. Nav items mirror the Go admin.\n  # Works for both LiveView sockets (assign_new/3 with change tracking) and dead\n  # controller renders (plain map — use Map.put_new/3 for compatibility).\n  def live(assigns) do\n    assigns =\n      if is_map_key(assigns, :__changed__) do\n        assigns\n        |> assign_new(:active_nav, fn -> nil end)\n        |> assign_new(:bootstrap_completed, fn -> true end)\n      else\n        assigns\n        |> Map.put_new(:active_nav, nil)\n        |> Map.put_new(:bootstrap_completed, true)\n      end\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <div\n      :if={is_map_key(assigns, :__changed__)}\n      id=\"admin-session-keepalive\"\n      phx-hook=\"SessionKeepalive\"\n      class=\"hidden\"\n      aria-hidden=\"true\"\n    >\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"min-h-screen flex flex-col\">\n      <header class=\"h-12 bg-base-300 flex items-center justify-between px-4 shrink-0\" role=\"banner\">\n        <span class=\"font-semibold text-base-content\">Nebu Admin</span>\n      </header>\n\n      <div class=\"flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n        <nav\n          aria-label=\"Admin navigation\"\n          class=\"w-60 bg-base-200 flex flex-col shrink-0 border-r border-base-300\"\n        >\n          <ul role=\"list\" class=\"flex flex-col gap-1 p-2 flex-1\">\n            <.nav_item :if={not @bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/bootstrap\" key=\"bootstrap\" active={@active_nav}>\n              Bootstrap Setup\n            </.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/dashboard\" key=\"dashboard\" active={@active_nav}>Dashboard</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/compliance\" key=\"compliance\" active={@active_nav}>Compliance</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} key=\"logs\" active={@active_nav} children={logs_nav_children()}>Logs</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/users\" key=\"users\" active={@active_nav}>Users</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/rooms\" key=\"rooms\" active={@active_nav}>Rooms</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} navigate=\"/admin/spaces\" key=\"spaces\" active={@active_nav}>Spaces</.nav_item>\n            <.nav_item :if={@bootstrap_completed} key=\"config\" active={@active_nav} children={config_nav_children()}>Configuration</.nav_item>\n          </ul>\n          <div :if={@bootstrap_completed} class=\"p-2 border-t border-base-300\">\n            <a\n              href=\"/admin/logout\"\n              data-navkey=\"logout\"\n              class=\"flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm w-full font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-base-200\"\n            >\n              Logout\n            </a>\n          </div>\n        </nav>\n\n        <main id=\"main-content\" class=\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-6\" role=\"main\">\n          {@inner_content}\n        </main>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  defp logs_nav_children, do: @logs_nav_children\n  defp config_nav_children, do: @config_nav_children\n\n  # A single sidebar nav entry, in one of two modes (admin-sidebar-nesting):\n  #\n  #   * LEAF (`children == []`, the default) — exactly the pre-nesting markup:\n  #     a plain `<li><.link data-navkey=...>`. Zero behavior change for every\n  #     existing call site that does not pass `:children` (AC2).\n  #\n  #   * PARENT (`children != []`) — an accessible disclosure group (AC9/AC10):\n  #     a real `<button aria-expanded aria-controls>` toggle (with a chevron\n  #     icon, AC13) followed by a nested, indented `<ul id=\"nav-{key}-children\">`\n  #     of child links. Children ALWAYS stay in the DOM; collapsing only adds\n  #     the boolean `hidden` attribute. The child `<ul>` must NOT carry any\n  #     display utility class (`flex`, `grid`, ...): an author-stylesheet\n  #     `.flex { display: flex }` rule beats the UA's `[hidden] { display:\n  #     none }` in the cascade and the collapsed state would not actually hide\n  #     (the exact trace-map-hierarchy cycle-1 bug — do not reintroduce it).\n  #\n  # Highlight contract (AC3/AC4):\n  #   * `key == active`            → primary highlight (leaf and parent alike)\n  #   * a child's `key == active`  → the child gets primary + aria-current=\"page\";\n  #                                  the parent gets the SECONDARY `bg-base-300`\n  #                                  accent (never `bg-primary`)\n  #   * otherwise                  → neutral (hover accent only)\n  #\n  # The expand/collapse behavior is wired CSP-safely (`script-src 'self'`, no\n  # inline JS) by `wireNavSectionToggles()` in priv/static/assets/app.js: a\n  # single delegated click listener flips `aria-expanded` + `hidden` on the\n  # native <button> — clicks, Space and Enter all fire `click` on a native\n  # button, and the delegation works identically on dead controller renders\n  # and LiveView pages. Sections default to EXPANDED (story Dev Notes Q3);\n  # collapse state deliberately does not persist across navigations (Q1).\n  #\n  # `:navigate` is LEAF-ONLY (code-review MINOR-3): a parent renders as a pure\n  # disclosure <button> that toggles and never navigates (AC9 + the keyboard\n  # ATDD contract — Space must flip aria-expanded WITHOUT leaving the page),\n  # so it has no use for a target. The attr is optional so parent call sites\n  # don't pass a meaningless value; every LEAF call site must still pass it —\n  # a leaf without `:navigate` would fall through to Phoenix.Component.link/1's\n  # dead `href=\"#\"` default.\n  attr :navigate, :string, default: nil\n  attr :key, :string, required: true\n  attr :active, :string, default: nil\n  attr :children, :list, default: []\n  attr :expanded, :boolean, default: true\n  slot :inner_block\n\n  def nav_item(%{children: []} = assigns) do\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <li>\n      <.link\n        navigate={@navigate}\n        data-navkey={@key}\n        aria-current={if @key == @active, do: \"page\"}\n        class={[\n          \"flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm\",\n          \"focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-base-200\",\n          if(@key == @active,\n            do: \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\",\n            else: \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n          )\n        ]}\n      >\n        {render_slot(@inner_block)}\n      </.link>\n    </li>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  def nav_item(assigns) do\n    highlight =\n      cond do\n        assigns.key == assigns.active -> \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\"\n        Enum.any?(assigns.children, &(&1.key == assigns.active)) -> \"bg-base-300 font-medium text-base-content\"\n        true -> \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n      end\n\n    assigns = assign(assigns, :parent_highlight, highlight)\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <li>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        data-navkey={@key}\n        data-nav-toggle\n        aria-expanded={to_string(@expanded)}\n        aria-controls={\"nav-#{@key}-children\"}\n        class={[\n          \"flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-3 rounded text-sm w-full text-left\",\n          \"focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-base-200\",\n          @parent_highlight\n        ]}\n      >\n        <svg\n          data-testid=\"nav-toggle-icon\"\n          class={[\"h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform\", @expanded && \"rotate-90\"]}\n          viewBox=\"0 0 20 20\"\n          fill=\"currentColor\"\n          aria-hidden=\"true\"\n        >\n          <path\n            fill-rule=\"evenodd\"\n            d=\"M7.21 14.77a.75.75 0 01.02-1.06L11.168 10 7.23 6.29a.75.75 0 111.04-1.08l4.5 4.25a.75.75 0 010 1.08l-4.5 4.25a.75.75 0 01-1.06-.02z\"\n            clip-rule=\"evenodd\"\n          />\n        </svg>\n        {render_slot(@inner_block)}\n      </button>\n      <ul id={\"nav-#{@key}-children\"} role=\"list\" class=\"ml-4 mt-1 space-y-1\" hidden={not @expanded}>\n        <li :for={child <- @children}>\n          <.link\n            navigate={child.navigate}\n            data-navkey={child.key}\n            aria-current={if child.key == @active, do: \"page\"}\n            class={[\n              \"flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded text-sm\",\n              \"focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-2 focus:ring-offset-base-200\",\n              if(child.key == @active,\n                do: \"bg-primary text-primary-content font-semibold\",\n                else: \"font-medium text-base-content hover:bg-base-300\"\n              )\n            ]}\n          >\n            {child.label}\n          </.link>\n        </li>\n      </ul>\n    </li>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\nend\n","numLines":234,"startLine":40,"totalLines":273}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"342b67cf-4c83-4d02-a850-81a8a95c13a3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

finding (blast-radius): `app.js`'s\n        `pushFilterSyncToServer/0` (fired from a WINDOW-level `popstate`\n        listener bound once at boot — see that function's own comment) needs\n        a `pushEvent` target element. {"parentUuid":"c967749b-6cb5-4bdf-bcc8-297106c9c7fb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"6ec863c2-24c3-45ae-8423-8a0d3113b5a0","agentId":"ae43d957caaab97ca","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KJUA2ffDHzseMAzo5TBFJN","type":"tool_result","content":"205\t    # Compliance\n206\t    get \"/compliance\", ComplianceController, :index\n207\t\n208\t    # Keepalive heartbeat (admin-liveview-session-refresh AC3/AC4): polled by a\n209\t    # client-side JS hook while any admin LiveView tab is open, so the existing\n210\t    # fetch_current_user/2 silent-refresh leg (which runs earlier in the :browser\n211\t    # pipeline, before this action) re-fires for otherwise-idle LiveView sockets.\n212\t    # Same admin auth gate as every other route (require_admin), minimal 204 response.\n213\t    get \"/keepalive\", KeepaliveController, :index\n214\t\n215\t    # ── Remaining LiveViews (real-time requirement) ────────────────────────────\n216\t    live_session :admin, on_mount: {Nebu.Admin.Auth, :require_admin} do\n217\t      live \"/dashboard\", DashboardLive, :index\n218\t      live \"/audit-log\", AuditLogLive, :index\n219\t      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream,\n220\t      # subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic. The embedded\n221\t      # Trace Map's Endpoint dimension was retired from this page by\n222\t      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC13) in favor of the dedicated\n223\t      # radial-map page below.\n224\t      live \"/logs\", LogsLive, :index\n225\t      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC1): the dedicated Trace Map\n226\t      # \"Leitstand\" page — a SEPARATE LiveView module (it defines\n227\t      # handle_params/3 for URL-driven filter state, which the shared\n228\t      # LogsLive must never export; see LogsLive.TraceMap's moduledoc).\n229\t      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive.TraceMap, :index\n230\t      # enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting (AC7 stub guidance): renders\n231\t      # the existing logs page so clicking the nav item never errors; the\n232\t      # real page lands in the `war-room-dedicated-page` story, which will\n233\t      # repoint this route.\n234\t      live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive, :index\n235\t    end\n236\t  end\n237\tend\n238\t"}]},"uuid":"2e77249b-61f3-401a-88e9-33595f15c0c2","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:27:48.681Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex","content":"    # Compliance\n    get \"/compliance\", ComplianceController, :index\n\n    # Keepalive heartbeat (admin-liveview-session-refresh AC3/AC4): polled by a\n    # client-side JS hook while any admin LiveView tab is open, so the existing\n    # fetch_current_user/2 silent-refresh leg (which runs earlier in the :browser\n    # pipeline, before this action) re-fires for otherwise-idle LiveView sockets.\n    # Same admin auth gate as every other route (require_admin), minimal 204 response.\n    get \"/keepalive\", KeepaliveController, :index\n\n    # ── Remaining LiveViews (real-time requirement) ────────────────────────────\n    live_session :admin, on_mount: {Nebu.Admin.Auth, :require_admin} do\n      live \"/dashboard\", DashboardLive, :index\n      live \"/audit-log\", AuditLogLive, :index\n      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream,\n      # subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic. The embedded\n      # Trace Map's Endpoint dimension was retired from this page by\n      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC13) in favor of the dedicated\n      # radial-map page below.\n      live \"/logs\", LogsLive, :index\n      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC1): the dedicated Trace Map\n      # \"Leitstand\" page — a SEPARATE LiveView module (it defines\n      # handle_params/3 for URL-driven filter state, which the shared\n      # LogsLive must never export; see LogsLive.TraceMap's moduledoc).\n      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive.TraceMap, :index\n      # enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting (AC7 stub guidance): renders\n      # the existing logs page so clicking the nav item never errors; the\n      # real page lands in the `war-room-dedicated-page` story, which will\n      # repoint this route.\n      live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive, :index\n    end\n  end\nend\n","numLines":34,"startLine":205,"totalLines":238}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c967749b-6cb5-4bdf-bcc8-297106c9c7fb","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a\n16\t  LiveView module makes every `live_isolated/3`-mounted test of that module\n17\t  fail at mount (empirically verified against the installed\n18\t  `phoenix_live_view` — see `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc). The\n19\t  existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (Live Stream page) has a large,\n20\t  deliberately-`live_isolated/3`-based suite, so the Leitstand gets its own\n21\t  module and `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` stays `handle_params/3`-free.\n22\t\n23\t  ## State model\n24\t\n25\t    * `@selected` — the active endpoint filters (satellite routes and/or\n26\t      domain keys), URL-driven: `handle_params/3` seeds it from the\n27\t      bracket-array `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` query params; every node\n28\t      click round-trips through `handle_event(\"toggle_node\"/\"clear_filters\")`\n29\t      -> `push_patch/2` -> `handle_params/3`. Bracket-array (not the story's\n30\t      literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`) per the story's own\n31\t      \"Story Amendments\": `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated\n32\t      bare keys, so the bare form cannot round-trip.\n33\t    * `@node_states` — one `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` state per map\n34\t      node (`\"nebu\"`, each domain key, each satellite route): the AC3\n35\t      60-second sliding window. Events are recorded at ALL THREE levels on"}]},"uuid":"0c994bee-a774-49da-892a-c8b613dace49","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:28:23.570Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — the dedicated, full-page radial health map at\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map` (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n  Visual reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n\n  ## Why this is a SEPARATE LiveView module, not a new action on `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n\n  AC5 hard-requires URL-driven filter state via `push_patch/2` +\n  `handle_params/3` (the lesson of trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix: filter\n  state must live in LiveView assigns seeded from the URL, NEVER in\n  client-DOM-only state). Merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a\n  LiveView module makes every `live_isolated/3`-mounted test of that module\n  fail at mount (empirically verified against the installed\n  `phoenix_live_view` — see `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc). The\n  existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (Live Stream page) has a large,\n  deliberately-`live_isolated/3`-based suite, so the Leitstand gets its own\n  module and `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` stays `handle_params/3`-free.\n\n  ## State model\n\n    * `@selected` — the active endpoint filters (satellite routes and/or\n      domain keys), URL-driven: `handle_params/3` seeds it from the\n      bracket-array `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` query params; every node\n      click round-trips through `handle_event(\"toggle_node\"/\"clear_filters\")`\n      -> `push_patch/2` -> `handle_params/3`. Bracket-array (not the story's\n      literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`) per the story's own\n      \"Story Amendments\": `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated\n      bare keys, so the bare form cannot round-trip.\n    * `@node_states` — one `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` state per map\n      node (`\"nebu\"`, each domain key, each satellite route): the AC3\n      60-second sliding window. Events are recorded at ALL THREE levels on","numLines":35,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1830}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fa272621-2119-4ac3-9586-aae344d7828e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"9089fb82-4e9f-477b-87ea-5167098458cc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"6ec863c2-24c3-45ae-8423-8a0d3113b5a0","agentId":"ae43d957caaab97ca","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XNqTcBehZmorvowLcvJmKP","type":"tool_result","content":"import Config\n\nconfig :logger, level: :warning\nconfig :nebu_http, port: 4001\n\n# enhanced-logging/matrix-payload-debug-logging (CI-gate cycle-2): disable\n# Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry's own Logger.debug/1 call in :test — the handler\n# still attaches and runs (unconditionally, everywhere), it just skips\n# logging. See the module's @moduledoc \"Disabled in :test\" section for why:\n# ExUnit.CaptureLog.capture_log/1 intercepts the GLOBAL logger, so a query\n# fired by any concurrently-running async test can otherwise inject an\n# unrelated log line into another test's captured output while it is\n# mid-capture at :debug (this broke request_logger_test.exs's AC1 JSON-line\n# extraction). No test asserts on this module's own output.\nconfig :nebu, Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry, enabled: false\n# oidc-middleware-migration: the JwksCache GenServer is NOT started from the\n# Application in test — jwks_cache_test.exs starts its own supervised instances\n# under the same registered name. Prod/dev start it (default true).\nconfig :nebu_http, start_jwks_cache: false\n# media-gateway-migration: the preview_url tests inject a fake fetch boundary, so\n# the real Finch pool is not needed in test (and starting it under the Application\n# would add an unused supervised process to the children-count assertions).\nconfig :nebu_http, start_finch: false\n\n# enhanced-logging/operational-log-store: the central :logger handler is NOT\n# attached automatically at boot in :test — every test that needs it\n# attached calls Nebu.Web.LogStore.attach_handler/0 itself (idempotent). This\n# keeps log_store_test.exs's \"registers exactly one new handler\" idempotency\n# test meaningful: it needs to observe a handler_ids delta of exactly +1 from\n# its OWN call, which a boot-time attach (already done before any test runs)\n# would otherwise pre-empt. Prod/dev attach it unconditionally at boot (AC2).\nconfig :nebu_http, attach_log_store_handler: false\nconfig :signature, pii_encryption_key: :crypto.strong_rand_bytes(32)\n\n# admin-ui-migration: the Phoenix LiveView Admin UI endpoint. The endpoint runs with\n# server: false (no port opened); it is started under the Application so the LiveView\n# unit test (admin_liveview_unit_test.exs) can mount RoomsLive via live_isolated/3,\n# which resolves the endpoint config (secret_key_base, signing salts). A fixed,\n# non-secret test key is used — this listener never faces the network in tests.\nconfig :nebu_http, start_admin_endpoint: true\n---\n7 matches in 3 files:\n\n156:0:nebu, Nebu.Repo),\n36:0:false` (server/config/config.exs) — Ecto's own\nlib/nebu/application.ex:43:Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry.attach()\nlib/nebu/application.ex:83:# enabled (and regardless of whether Nebu.Repo is configured at all —\nlib/nebu/application.ex:89:repo_children() ++\nlib/nebu/application.ex:155:defp repo_children do\nlib/nebu/application.ex:158:Nebu.Repo,","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"2020cf15-204a-4033-a3c1-a2330f56f608","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:30:57.657Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import Config\n\nconfig :logger, level: :warning\nconfig :nebu_http, port: 4001\n\n# enhanced-logging/matrix-payload-debug-logging (CI-gate cycle-2): disable\n# Nebu.Repo.QueryTelemetry's own Logger.debug/1 call in :test — the handler\n# still attaches and runs (unconditionally, everywhere), it just skips\n# logging. {"parentUuid":"38882ff4-3df1-4016-8216-5bc91aea2737","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"6ec863c2-24c3-45ae-8423-8a0d3113b5a0","agentId":"ae43d957caaab97ca","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018GeBzn4hSK4uNAVGoK2t32","type":"tool_result","content":"145\t  # owns the increment/timeout atomicity boundary) is started the same way,\n146\t  # right alongside Writer/PolicyCache — nothing to cache without a DB\n147\t  # either, and the same :permanent supervisor-restart contract applies (a\n148\t  # crashed WarRoom is restarted under the same registered name; its own\n149\t  # init/1 reloads the active session from the DB, immediately ending it if\n150\t  # it is already past its max_duration_s deadline — AC5 across a restart).\n151\t  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n152\t  # (caches which off-list endpoints have an active temporary capture, for\n153\t  # should_persist?/2's hot path) is started the same way — nothing to\n154\t  # cache without a DB either.\n155\t  defp repo_children do\n156\t    if Application.get_env(:nebu, Nebu.Repo),\n157\t      do: [\n158\t        Nebu.Repo,\n159\t        Nebu.Matrix.Session.Sweeper,\n160\t        Nebu.Matrix.Session.RefreshWorker,\n161\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer,\n162\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache,\n163\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom,\n164\t        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache"}]},"uuid":"bfedf9ae-aa15-4079-9824-9f259536669d","timestamp":"2026-07-08T10:31:02.253Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/application.ex","content":"  # owns the increment/timeout atomicity boundary) is started the same way,\n  # right alongside Writer/PolicyCache — nothing to cache without a DB\n  # either, and the same :permanent supervisor-restart contract applies (a\n  # crashed WarRoom is restarted under the same registered name; its own\n  # init/1 reloads the active session from the DB, immediately ending it if\n  # it is already past its max_duration_s deadline — AC5 across a restart).\n  # enhanced-logging/trace-map-node-controls: Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache\n  # (caches which off-list endpoints have an active temporary capture, for\n  # should_persist?/2's hot path) is started the same way — nothing to\n  # cache without a DB either.\n  defp repo_children do\n    if Application.get_env(:nebu, Nebu.Repo),\n      do: [\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        Nebu.Matrix.Session.Sweeper,\n        Nebu.Matrix.Session.RefreshWorker,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.Writer,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.PolicyCache,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom,\n        Nebu.Web.LogStore.CaptureCache","numLines":20,"startLine":145,"totalLines":225}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"38882ff4-3df1-4016-8216-5bc91aea2737","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Test counts stated in the task match the\nfiles exactly (verified via script: `war_room_test.exs` is 13 unit + 9\n`:integration` = 22; other files 4/4/5/7/8 respectively; **9**, not 10,\n`Scenario:` blocks in the feature file — see MINOR-8).\n\n---\n\n## AC / Amendment coverage matrix (all ✅, no MAJOR gap)\n\nAC1–AC10, AC12–AC14 each have ≥1 test in\n`server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room_test.exs`. AC11 (design decision:\n\"exclusive scope only for War Room, monitoring-page generalization out of\nscope\") correctly has no test — it's a documented non-requirement, not a gap.\nAmendments 1–6 each have dedicated tests (amendment 1 →\n`war_room_test.exs`'s \"amendment 1\" describe block; amendment 2 →\n`war_room_panel_retirement_test.exs`; amendment 3 →\n`trace_map_bubble_readonly_mode_test.exs` + the \"amendment 3\" describe in\n`war_room_test.exs`; amendment 4 → `layouts_war_room_indicator_test.exs`;\namendment 5 → reused in `war_room_test.exs` AC9 test, `war_room_manager_exclusive_scope_test.exs`'s \"amendment 5\" describe, and the e2e steps; amendment 6 →\nthe AC14/amendment-6 describe block). Amendment 7 (security review stays\nrequired) is a process gate, not directly testable — partially addressed by\nthe defense-in-depth shape-validation tests (see deviation 4 below).\n\n---\n\n## The 5 flagged ATDD deviations — evaluated\n\n1. **Single `LogsLive.WarRoom` module, no `handle_params/3` — CORRECT.**\n   Verified against `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex` (current stub:\n   `live \"/logs/war-room\", LogsLive, :index\"`, no `:room_id` route yet) and\n   `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex`'s own moduledoc, which\n   documents that *exporting* `handle_params/3` breaks `live_isolated/3`\n   mounts, and that TraceMap needs it only because its endpoint filter is\n   bookmarkable via `push_patch/2` (bracket-array query params). WarRoom's\n   `:show` uses `<.link navigate=...>`, which Phoenix LiveView treats as a\n   full remount (unmount/mount), so `mount/3` genuinely receives fresh\n   `%{\"room_id\" => id}` params every time — `handle_params/3` would be dead\n   code here. The right call, correctly reasoned, and the tests correctly\n   split list-mode (`live_isolated/3`) from detail-mode/route-repoint\n   (`mount_via_router`) accordingly.\n\n2. **Migration number free — CONFIRMED.** `server/priv/migrations/` tops out\n   at `000020_admin_temporary_captures.{up,down}.sql`; `000021` is free. The\n   schema-contract test in `war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs` pins a\n   nullable `jsonb` column via `information_schema.columns`, satisfiable by a\n   plain `ALTER TABLE war_room_sessions ADD COLUMN exclusive_scope JSONB`\n   (Dev Notes Option A) — no conflict.\n\n3. **Elixir 1.20 type-checker noise on `start_session/1` — plausible, but\n   unverifiable pre-implementation.** `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_manager.ex`'s\n   `@spec start_session(map()) :: {:ok, map()} | ...` uses the fully dynamic\n   `map()` type for both input and the `{:ok, ...}` return — no shape\n   constraint exists to violate by adding an `exclusive_scope` key. The claim\n   is credible given current code, but since the implementation (and thus\n   any real compiler/dialyzer run) doesn't exist yet, this can't be\n   definitively confirmed non-fatal until the dev-story lands and compiles\n   with `--warnings-as-errors`. INFO, not a blocker.\n\n4. **AC9 shape validation vs. closed allowlist — reasonable resolution, with\n   one gap.** A closed allowlist would contradict AC9's explicit \"plus an\n   'other' option to type a custom route\" requirement, so shape-only\n   validation (non-empty string, list, starts with `/`) is the correct\n   compromise, and `war_room_manager_exclusive_scope_test.exs` pins rejection\n   of non-list/non-string/blank/non-path entries well (including an\n   attempted SQL-injection-shaped string, though all DB writes are\n   parameterized regardless so that specific test is more \"shape sanity\"\n   than a real injection backstop). **Gap**: no test anywhere asserts that an\n   admin-supplied `exclusive_scope`/`exclusive_other` value is safely\n   HTML-escaped wherever it's later rendered (e.g. a session's scope\n   description on the list/detail view) — given `security_review: required`\n   and Dev Notes item 7's explicit \"don't trust the form's UI validation\n   alone\" framing, this is worth pinning now rather than leaving to\n   Kassandra to discover post-hoc. Classified MINOR (speculative — no\n   confirmed unsafe render path exists yet since the page isn't built), not\n   MAJOR, but flagged for the dev-story/Kassandra explicitly.\n\n5. **`@copy_toast` instead of `@flash` — CONFIRMED.** `grep` of\n   `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex` returns zero matches for\n   `flash`/`@flash` — the shared admin layout genuinely never renders it, so\n   a dedicated `@copy_toast` assign (mirroring `LogsLive.TraceMap`'s\n   `@cascade_flash` precedent, confirmed to exist) is the only workable\n   design. Correct.\n\n---\n\n## Standard checks\n\n- **Determinism**: no hard waits found (`grep` for `waitForTimeout`,\n  `Process.sleep`, `:timer.sleep` across all 7 new test/step files returned\n  only a comment stating the *absence* of such waits). PubSub-then-`render/1`\n  and GenServer `:sys.get_state` synchronization patterns are used correctly\n  throughout — no flakiness risk identified.\n- **Selectors**: consistently `data-testid`/`data-navkey`/ARIA-attribute\n  based; the handful of literal-copy-string assertions\n  (`refute html =~ \"🚨 Start War Room\"`, `refute html =~ \"Sammlung\n  konfigurieren\"`) are deliberate absence-proofs mirroring the codebase's own\n  `trace_map_retirement_test.exs` precedent, not brittle happy-path text\n  matching.\n- **GenServer crash/restart**: `Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom` already has a\n  crash/restart test in the pre-existing (unstaged)\n  `server/test/nebu/web/log_store/war_room_test.exs` (`describe \"crash/restart\n  via supervisor (AC5 boot-time load...\")`). This story doesn't introduce a\n  new GenServer, only a new column/branch in `check/1` — see MINOR-5 below\n  for the one related gap.\n- **UI story → Playwright tests present**: yes,\n  `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature` (9 `@pending-feature`\n  scenarios) + `e2e/step-definitions/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.steps.ts` +\n  extended `e2e/fixtures/db.ts` (SQL-only seeding, no test-only HTTP routes,\n  matching epic convention).\n- **Matrix spec/HTTP codes**: N/A — `matrix: false` in frontmatter, no Matrix\n  C-S API surface touched by this story.\n- **Embedded-panel-retirement test genuinely proves absence, not vacuous**:\n  confirmed — `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex:1007` currently\n  unconditionally renders `<WarRoomPanel.war_room_panel .../>`, so all 4\n  retirement tests are genuinely RED today, not trivially-true refutes.\n- **AC12/amendment-1 stub-route-repoint test distinguishes new page from old\n  mislabel**: confirmed — it asserts `h1 =~ \"War Room\"` **and** refutes the\n  Live-Stream-only string `\"Sammlung konfigurieren\"`, plus separately asserts\n  `active_nav` genuinely becomes `\"logs-war-room\"` (not stuck on\n  `\"logs-monitoring\"`). Non-vacuous.\n- **Exclusive-scope logic tests both inclusion and exclusion**: confirmed —\n  `war_room_exclusive_scope_test.exs` covers excluded-route (not tagged),\n  non-excluded-route (tagged+counted), multiple excluded routes, AND-combination\n  with an existing `filter`, and nil/`[]` backward-compat no-op paths.\n\n---\n\n## MINOR findings\n\n1. **AC9's free-text \"other\" input not exercised end-to-end at the LiveView\n   level.** `war_room_test.exs`'s AC10 test only submits a pre-built\n   `exclusive_scope` list (checkbox path); `war_room_manager_exclusive_scope_test.exs`'s\n   \"other\" test calls `WarRoomManager.start_session/1` *directly*, bypassing\n   the form's own merge-in logic for `exclusive_other`. No test proves the\n   LiveView actually reads the `exclusive_other` input and appends it to the\n   submitted list.\n2. **AC5's \"sorted by timestamp\" is unverified.** The detail-view test\n   asserts row count (5) and cross-session exclusion, but never asserts\n   chronological ordering of the rendered rows — a reversed or unsorted\n   implementation would still pass.\n3. **Copy-to-markdown format is only substring-checked**, not validated\n   against Dev Notes item 5's actual Markdown template (`**Started by:**`,\n   `## Captured Logs`, `- \\`{timestamp}\\` [{level}] [{source}] {message}`\n   bullet format). A non-Markdown plain-text dump containing the same values\n   would still pass.\n4. **Mislabeled describe block**: `war_room_test.exs` line 733,\n   `describe \"AC7/AC11: broad/noisy-scope warnings...\"` — should read\n   `AC7/AC8` (the story's own Acceptance Test #11 citation is \"[AC7–AC8]\n   Warnings...\"; AC11 is the unrelated monitoring-page-generalization design\n   decision). Risk of confusing a future AC-coverage audit.\n5. **No crash/restart test specific to `exclusive_scope` reload.** The\n   pre-existing `WarRoom` GenServer crash/restart test predates this\n   column; nothing confirms `exclusive_scope` survives a supervisor restart\n   (reload-from-DB) correctly, only that it's read/matched in the live\n   process.\n6. **No keyboard-interaction Playwright scenario** for the new list/detail\n   pages or the scoping form's exclusive-scope checkboxes, despite the\n   codebase's own `admin-sidebar-nesting` precedent of pinning explicit\n   Tab-order/keyboard-activation E2E scenarios for new interactive nav\n   elements. Native `<a>`/`<button>` usage (verified via the AC3 `href`\n   assertion) gives baseline operability, but it's untested end-to-end.\n7. **Weak/near-vacuous assertion** in the `amendment2-embedded-panel-retired`\n   scenario: `\"a link to the dedicated War Room page is visible on\n   {string}\"` checks the sidebar's `a[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]`, which\n   already exists today (shipped by `admin-sidebar-nesting`), so this step\n   would pass even without any of this story's changes — it doesn't actually\n   validate anything new introduced by amendment 2.\n8. **Scenario count mismatch**: `grep -c \"^  Scenario:\"` on\n   `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature` returns **9**, not\n   the \"10\" claimed both in the task framing and in\n   `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/pipeline-state.yaml`'s own ATDD\n   log line (\"22+25 integration red + **10** E2E scenarios\"). Worth\n   reconciling — possibly an intended AC13/accessibility or AC11/warnings\n   scenario was dropped.\n9. **No test pins safe rendering of admin-supplied `exclusive_scope`\n   values** (see deviation 4 above) — a cheap, security-review-relevant\n   assertion to add given this story's `security_review: required` status.\n\n## INFO notes\n\n- Deviations 1, 2, and 5 were independently verified against the current\n  source tree and are architecturally sound / accurately claimed.\n- Deviation 3 is plausible but could not be verified without a real compiler\n  run (no implementation
